# Rootfix Audit

- Generated: 2026-10-10T14:12:34.012192+00:00
- Request SHA-256: `9999991ca0962d83113a5bc41b956a982d222dd42bd76449c8d2c911a28d0898`
- Repository: `/Users/maxboving/projects/usedSurf`

## Request

Refactor board assignment so inventory cards are hard identity anchors, board-specific evidence is more important than sequence, and contradictory or weakly supported photos remain unassigned for review instead of being silently forced into a group. Preserve optional angle-aware evidence: unsupported fields remain unknown, explicit contradictions are rejected, and visual grouping must not manufacture membership merely to reach the card count.

### general:grouping.truthful

**Law:** Code generated should not silently assign a photo to a board when the available evidence is below the grouping threshold.
**Enforcement:** missing_negative_tests: pass, negative_case_tests: pass
**Evidence:** organizer/ingest.py (test: python3 -m unittest tests.test_grouping.GroupingTests.test_weak_card_match_is_not_forced)

**Relevant edit excerpts:**

`organizer/ingest.py`

```diff

@@ -9,6 +9,7 @@ from pathlib import Path
 from PIL import Image, ImageOps
 
 from .card_ocr import extract_card_identifier
+from .board_evidence import evidence_fields_for_shot
 
 try:

@@ -29,13 +30,31 @@ except ImportError:
     pass

 MAX_PHOTOS_PER_BOARD = 7
 STANDARD_BOARD_PHOTOS = 6
 SHOT_LIMITS = {"full_board": 4, "side_profile": 1, "fin_detail": 1, "card": 1}
 BOARD_SIMILARITY_BOUNDARY = 0.10
 LOCAL_NEIGHBOR_COUNT = 8
 LOCAL_TIME_WINDOW_SECONDS = 10 * 60
+CARD_MATCH_MAX_MINUTES = 8
+CARD_MATCH_MAX_VISUAL_DISTANCE = 0.35
+BOUNDARY_REFINEMENT_RADIUS = 3
+IDENTITY_ASSIGNMENT_MAX_DISTANCE = 0.42
+IDENTITY_ASSIGNMENT_MIN_MARGIN = 0.035
+_LEARNED_BOARD_IDENTITY_WEIGHTS: dict[str, float] | None = None
+
+
+def set_board_identity_weights(weights: dict[str, float] | None) -> None:
+    """Set the optional locally learned metric used by future grouping runs."""
+    global _LEARNED_BOARD_IDENTITY_WEIGHTS
+    if not weights:
+        _LEARNED_BOARD_IDENTITY_WEIGHTS = None
+        return
+    _LEARNED_BOARD_IDENTITY_WEIGHTS = {
+        name: max(0.0, float(weights.get(name, 0.0)))
+        for name in ("color", "layout", "edges", "geometry", "shot")
+    }
 
 
 def visual_signature(image: Image.Image) -> tuple[float, ...]:
@@ -61,15 +80,241 @@ def visual_signature(image: Image.Image) -> tuple[float, ...]:
     # boards, but retain chroma because different board colors are meaningful.
     lab[:, :, 0] = (lab[:, :, 0] - float(lab[:, :, 0].mean())) / max(float(lab[:, :, 0].std()), 0.04)
     small = cv2.resize(lab, (12, 18), interpolation=cv2.INTER_AREA)
+    gray = cv2.cvtColor(roi, cv2.COLOR_RGB2GRAY).astype(np.float32) / 255.0
+    gray = (gray - float(gray.mean())) / max(float(gray.std()), 0.08)
+    small_gray = cv2.resize(gray, (12, 18), interpolation=cv2.INTER_AREA)
+    edges = cv2.Canny((np.clip(gray, -2.0, 2.0) * 40.0 + 128.0).astype(np.uint8), 40, 120)
+    small_edges = cv2.resize(edges, (12, 18), interpolation=cv2.INTER_AREA).astype(np.float32) / 255.0
     summary = np.concatenate([
         lab.mean(axis=(0, 1)),
         lab.std(axis=(0, 1)),
         small[:, :, 1:].reshape(-1),
+        small_gray.reshape(-1),
+        small_edges.reshape(-1),
     ])
     return tuple(float(value) for value in summary)
 
 
+def board_identity_signature(
+    image: Image.Image,
+    candidate: tuple[int, int, int, int] | None = None,
+    shot_type: str = "",
+) -> tuple[float, ...]:
+    """Describe the board itself after normalizing away the room.
+
+    The grouping fingerprint is intentionally made from the detected board
+    crop, not the whole camera frame. It combines color distribution, a
+    normalized grayscale layout, edge/graphic layout, and coarse geometry.
+    Geometry is kept as a small final section so a side profile can still be
+    compared to the same board's full-board views without dominating identity.
+    """
+    if cv2 is None or np is None:
+        return ()
+    working = image.convert("RGB")
+    width, height = working.size
+    geometry = (0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0)
+    if candidate and len(candidate) == 4:
+        x, y, box_width, box_height = (int(value) for value in candidate)
+        if box_width > 2 and box_height > 2:
+            pad = round(max(box_width, box_height) * 0.08)
+            left, top = max(0, x - pad), max(0, y - pad)
+            right = min(width, x + box_width + pad)
+            bottom = min(height, y + box_height + pad)
+            working = working.crop((left, top, right, bottom))
+            geometry = (
+                box_width / max(width, 1),
+                box_height / max(height, 1),
+                (x + box_width / 2) / max(width, 1),
+                (y + box_height / 2) / max(height, 1),
+            )
+    else:
+        # Keep the fallback compatible with scan-only callers. Production
+        # grouping replaces this with the classifier's board candidate.
+        left, right = round(width * 0.16), round(width * 0.84)
+        top, bottom = round(height * 0.08), round(height * 0.92)
+        working = working.crop((left, top, right, bottom))
+    working.thumbnail((480, 640))
+    array = np.asarray(working)
+    if array.size == 0:
+        return ()
+    hsv = cv2.cvtColor(array, cv2.COLOR_RGB2HSV)
+    histogram = cv2.calcHist([hsv], [0, 1], None, [12, 4], [0, 180, 0, 256]).flatten().astype(np.float32)
+    histogram /= max(float(np.linalg.norm(histogram)), 1.0)
+    gray = cv2.cvtColor(array, cv2.COLOR_RGB2GRAY).astype(np.float32) / 255.0
+    gray = (gray - float(gray.mean())) / max(float(gray.std()), 0.08)
+    gray_small = cv2.resize(gray, (20, 32), interpolation=cv2.INTER_AREA).reshape(-1)
+    edges = cv2.
```

### feature:grouping.angle-aware-evidence

**Law:** Code generated should not infer an identity field from an angle that cannot reliably expose that field.
**Enforcement:** missing_negative_tests: pass, negative_case_tests: pass
**Evidence:** organizer/board_evidence.py (test: python3 -m unittest tests.test_board_evidence.BoardEvidenceTests.test_evidence_is_angle_specific_and_optional)

**Relevant edit excerpts:**

`organizer/board_evidence.py`

```diff
+++ organizer/board_evidence.py (untracked)
+"""Field-level board evidence, kept separate from grouping ownership.
+
+Grouping answers "which photos belong together?" This module answers "what
+does that board appear to be?" It never turns missing evidence into a
+negative and keeps the inventory card as a source, not an unquestioned truth.
+"""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import re
+from collections import Counter
+from difflib import SequenceMatcher
+
+from PIL import Image
+
+from .card_ocr import extract_card_identifier, normalize_ocr_text
+
+try:
+    import cv2
+    import numpy as np
+except ImportError:
+    cv2 = None
+    np = None
+
+
+FIELDS = ("brand", "model", "fin_system", "fin_box_count", "fins_included")
+SHOT_FIELD_ELIGIBILITY = {
+    "card": set(FIELDS),
+    # Full-board/deck views are the useful angle for markings and boxes.
+    "full_board": {"brand", "model", "fin_system", "fin_box_count"},
+    # A profile can show whether fins are physically installed, but not
+    # reliably identify the board's markings or box count.
+    "side_profile": {"fins_included"},
+    "fin_detail": {"fin_system", "fin_box_count", "fins_included"},
+}
+_FIN_SYSTEM_RE = re.compile(r"\b(FCS\s*(?:II|I|2)?|FUTURES?)\b", re.I)
+
+
+def _compact(value: str) -> str:
+    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]+", "", str(value).casefold())
+
+
+def _matches(expected: str, text: str) -> bool:
+    expected_key = _compact(expected)
+    text_key = _compact(text)
+    if not expected_key or not text_key:
+        return False
+    if expected_key in text_key:
+        return True
+    return SequenceMatcher(None, expected_key, text_key).ratio() >= 0.86
+
+
+def extract_photo_evidence(text: str, shot_type: str = "") -> dict[str, dict]:
+    """Extract only explicit text evidence from a board photo.
+
+    A fin/profile frame is useful context, but its mere existence does not
+    prove that fins are included. That distinction is intentional.
+    """
+    identifier = extract_card_identifier(text) if text else {}
+    fin_match = _FIN_SYSTEM_RE.search(normalize_ocr_text(text)) if text else None
+    if fin_match and "fin_system" not in identifier:
+        identifier["fin_system"] = "FCS II" if fin_match.group(1).upper().startswith("FCS") else "Futures"
+    evidence = {}
+    eligible = SHOT_FIELD_ELIGIBILITY.get(shot_type, set())
+    for field, value in identifier.items():
+        if field in eligible and value:
+            evidence[field] = {"value": value, "source": "photo_ocr", "shot_type": shot_type, "confidence": 0.62}
+    return evidence
+
+
+def evidence_fields_for_shot(shot_type: str) -> set[str]:
+    """Return fields this angle is allowed to assert."""
+    return set(SHOT_FIELD_ELIGIBILITY.get(shot_type, set()))
+
+
+def detect_side_profile_fins(image: Image.Image, candidate: tuple[int, int, int, int] | None = None) -> dict[str, object]:
+    """Return conservative visual fins evidence for a side/profile frame.
+
+    A profile is only positive when several dark, compact protrusions appear
+    immediately outside the lower board edge. Everything else remains
+    unknown; lighting, stands, rails, and shadows must not become a false
+    ``fins included=no`` conclusion.
+    """
+    if cv2 is None or np is None or not candidate or len(candidate) != 4:
+        return {"value": "", "status": "unknown", "confidence": 0.0, "source": "side_profile_visual"}
+    x, y, width, height = (int(value) for value in candidate)
+    if width < 3 or height < 3:
+        return {"value": "", "status": "unknown", "confidence": 0.0, "source": "side_profile_visual"}
+    array = cv2.cvtColor(np.asarray(image.convert("RGB")), cv2.COLOR_RGB2GRAY)
+    image_height, image_width = array.shape[:2]
+    left, right = max(0, x), min(image_width, x + width)
+    top, bottom = max(0, y), min(image_height, y + height)
+    if right <= left or bottom <= top:
+        return {"value": "", "status": "unknown", "confidence": 0.0, "source": "side_profile_visual"}
+    board = array[top:bottom, left:right]
+    # Fins are expected near the tail, not throughout the whole profile.
+    tail = board[round(board.shape[0] * 0.62):]
+    if tail.size == 0:
+        return {"value": "", "status": "unknown", "confidence": 0.0, "source": "side_profile_visual"}
+    threshold = float(np.percentile(tail, 22))
+    mask = (tail <= threshold).astype(np.uint8) * 255
+    mask = cv2.morphologyEx(mask, cv2.MORPH_OPEN, np.ones((3, 3), np.uint8))
+    contours, _ = cv2.findContours(mask, cv2.RETR_EXTERNAL, cv2.CHAIN_APPROX_SIMPLE)
+    compact = []
+    for contour in contours:
+        cx, cy, cw, ch = cv2.boundingRect(contour)
+        area = cv2.contourArea(contour)
+        if area >= max(4.0, board.size * 0.0008) and ch >= max(3, tail.shape[0] * 0.08) and cw <= board.shape[1] * 0.45:
+            compact.append((cx, cy, cw, ch, area))
+    if len(compact) >= 2:
+        confidence = min(0.88, 0.55 + len(compact) * 0.08)
+        return {"value"
```
